## 🧭 Reflow Risk (WCAG 1.4.10)

Page-level indicator: resizes to 320 CSS pixels wide and checks for horizontal overflow. This is a risk indicator, not a conformance verdict — see [Behavioral Accessibility Automation](https://github.com/mgifford/ACCESSIBILITY.md/blob/main/examples/BEHAVIORAL_ACCESSIBILITY_AUTOMATION.md) for what it can and cannot detect.

| Page | Result | Details | Page Title |
|---|---|---|---|
| [View Page](https://huggingface.co/) | failed | Horizontal overflow detected: 33px of content overflowed the viewport at 320px width | Hugging Face – The AI community building the future. |

## 🔍 Cross-Page Patterns: Common HTML Issues

These HTML patterns cause the same accessibility errors across multiple pages. **Fix the pattern once in your codebase to fix it everywhere!**

### 🎯 Top Patterns to Fix (Highest Impact)

#### Pattern 1: Affects 20 page(s) - 40 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: axe
**Rule**: [button-name](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.14/button-name?application=playwright)
**Impact**: critical
**Issue**: Buttons must have discernible text

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<button type="button" class="px-2 py-0.5 hover:text-gray-500 dark:hover:text-gray-600 flex items-center
			" aria-expanded="false">
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
.dark\:hover\:text-gray-600
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('.dark\:hover\:text-gray-600')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:
- https://huggingface.co
- https://huggingface.co/blog/flux-3
- https://huggingface.co/blog/open-llm-leaderboard-mmlu
- https://huggingface.co/blog/Hcompany/holotab
- https://huggingface.co/blog/1_58_llm_extreme_quantization
- *...and 15 more page(s)*

---

#### Pattern 2: Affects 20 page(s) - 40 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: axe
**Rule**: [color-contrast](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.14/color-contrast?application=playwright)
**Impact**: serious
**Issue**: Elements must meet minimum color contrast ratio thresholds

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<kbd aria-hidden="true" class="ml-auto flex-none whitespace-nowrap rounded-sm bg-gray-100 px-1.5 font-mono text-xs text-gray-400 dark:bg-gray-800 dark:text-gray-500 max-md:hidden">Ctrl K</kbd>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
kbd
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('kbd')`
5. The element will be highlighted

#### Pattern 3: Affects 20 page(s) - 40 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: axe
**Rule**: [meta-viewport](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.14/meta-viewport?application=playwright)
**Impact**: moderate
**Issue**: Zooming and scaling must not be disabled

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no">
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
meta[name="viewport"]

```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
